# Skills · D · part 11

859 entries, db-referential-integrity to dcm-doctor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **db-referential-integrity**: Audit foreign-key integrity — missing FKs that allow orphan rows (financial/auth = severity 5), absent or unsafe ON DELETE/ON UPDATE actions, reference cycles, and composite-FK column/order mismatches. Module M3. Feeds both the Design & Integrity (Integridad referencial) and Performance & Scale (Query) scores. ([Hainrixz/claude-db](https://github.com/Hainrixz/claude-db/tree/HEAD/skills/db-referential-integrity) · ★19)
- **db-relational-design**: Design relational schemas — entities, relations, keys, indexes, constraints — with normalisation, performance, and migration planning. ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/db/db-relational-design))
- **db-reliability**: Operate the database reliably: pool connections with sane limits, choose the isolation level deliberately (knowing its anomalies), keep transactions short, route replica reads carefully, and test restores — a backup is only real if you've restored it. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/database-engineering/skills/db-reliability) · ★7)
- **db-repair**: Auto-fix gbrain's Postgres access so the brain stays available. - **dba-mongo**: Use whenever the task involves MongoDB 7+ database design or administration: document schema design, ESR index rule, aggregation pipeline construction, sharding strategy, Atlas Search, change streams. - **dba-mongodb**: MongoDB heuristics catalogue for the dba role. Detects indexing (ESR rule), modeling (embed vs reference), query, migration, retention (TTL), capacity (shard key), security, and concurrency problems. Every finding ships with a Mongo shell command. ([leocosta/octopus](https://github.com/leocosta/octopus/tree/HEAD/skills/dba-mongodb) · ★6)
- **dba-mssql**: MSSQL heuristics catalogue for the dba role. Detects indexing, modeling, query, migration, retention, capacity, security, and concurrency problems specific to SQL Server. Every finding ships with a T-SQL fix. ([leocosta/octopus](https://github.com/leocosta/octopus/tree/HEAD/skills/dba-mssql) · ★6)
- **dba-mssql**: Use whenever the task involves SQL Server 2022+ database design or administration: T-SQL stored procedures and functions, DMV performance monitoring, columnstore indexes, temporal tables, Always On availability groups. - **dba-mysql**: Use whenever the task involves MySQL 8.0/MariaDB 11 database design or administration: InnoDB schema design, Flyway migrations, EXPLAIN FORMAT=TREE analysis, full-text search, covering indexes, replication. - **DBT个案概念化三步闭环协议**: 一个结构化、可迭代、符合DBT辩证逻辑的宏观治疗框架，用于系统化组织临床信息、确立目标层级并基于实证反馈动态调整治疗方案。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E4%B8%AA%E6%A1%88%E6%A6%82%E5%BF%B5%E5%8C%96%E4%B8%89%E6%AD%A5%E9%97%AD%E7%8E%AF%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★587)
- **DBT五模块结构化节奏规划协议**: 依据问题形成与解决的时序逻辑，将12次50分钟DBT咨询划分为两阶段强度节奏：前4周（8次）双频次聚焦阶段1–2技能奠基；第4周起单频次推进阶段3–5并同步强化生活迁移，确保技能从咨询室向真实高频情境系统性泛化。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E4%BA%94%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E8%8A%82%E5%A5%8F%E8%A7%84%E5%88%92%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★587)
- **DBT五阶段目标共建协议**: 基于DBT框架，与青少年来访者在首次关系建立后协作完成结构化、递进式五阶段目标设定：每阶段严格配对‘减少1个可观测不适行为’与‘增加1个DBT核心技能’，最终锚定于自我认同强化与复原力提升。产出双方签署的可测量、可练习、可验证的目标作业单。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E4%BA%94%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%9B%AE%E6%A0%87%E5%85%B1%E5%BB%BA%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★587)
- **DBT五阶段结构化咨询框架**: 一个基于辩证行为疗法（DBT）的跨阶段、可复用的长程个体咨询全流程骨架，覆盖关系建立、活在当下、问题链分析、危机生存技能训练到生涯整合与结束，强调协同作业、具身化产出与效果锚定。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E4%BA%94%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★587)
- **DBT四模块结构化导入会谈**: 在首次咨询中，以非评判、具身化、生活化语言系统介绍DBT四大核心模块（正念、人际效能、情绪调节、痛苦耐受），依托标准化讲义进行视觉协同，锚定来访者当前困扰，激发技能习得动机，建立治疗同盟与结构化干预共识。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E5%9B%9B%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%AF%BC%E5%85%A5%E4%BC%9A%E8%B0%88) · ★587)
- **DBT团体准备中的技能锚定安抚**: 当来访者对首次参与DBT团体表达社交焦虑与回避倾向时，用于将具体DBT技能与团体情境不适感直接锚定，提供即时可用的心理支持脚手架。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E5%9B%A2%E4%BD%93%E5%87%86%E5%A4%87%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E9%94%9A%E5%AE%9A%E5%AE%89%E6%8A%9A) · ★587)
- **DBT团体前访谈结构**: 在DBT日间病房治疗启动前，通过50分钟一对一访谈建立治疗联盟、完成个案概念化、介绍DBT框架，并协商进入团体技能训练的承诺。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E5%9B%A2%E4%BD%93%E5%89%8D%E8%AE%BF%E8%B0%88%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84) · ★587)
- **DBT团体技能训练八周结构化流程**: 一个标准化的八周DBT团体技能训练课程框架，按周组织正念、痛苦耐受、情绪调节、人际效能四大模块，每节课含明确主题、技能名称与实操内容。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E5%9B%A2%E4%BD%93%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83%E5%85%AB%E5%91%A8%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★587)
- **DBT技巧四步闭环教学**: 在单次咨询中，以结构化、协作式、行为导向方式教授一个DBT核心技巧（正念/人际效能/情绪调节/痛苦耐受），通过讲解-示范-角色扮演-家庭作业四步闭环，确保来访者完成一次可观察的行为练习并提交具象化作业。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E6%8A%80%E5%B7%A7%E5%9B%9B%E6%AD%A5%E9%97%AD%E7%8E%AF%E6%95%99%E5%AD%A6) · ★587)
- **DBT日记卡功能澄清与情绪联想重构**: 当来访者将日记卡体验为‘作业’并引发烦躁、回避等强烈负性情绪反应时，用于澄清日记卡的观察性功能，并协助来访者解构其与过往创伤性学业经历的情绪联结。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E6%97%A5%E8%AE%B0%E5%8D%A1%E5%8A%9F%E8%83%BD%E6%BE%84%E6%B8%85%E4%B8%8E%E6%83%85%E7%BB%AA%E8%81%94%E6%83%B3%E9%87%8D%E6%9E%84) · ★587)
- **DBT生物社会理论 psychoeducation 模块化讲义交付**: 在来访者初步接纳自身困境后，引入‘解决任何问题的选项讲义’与‘技巧训练假设讲义’，拓展其行为选择意识与改变可能性信念。适用于来访者陷入‘无解感’、行为僵化或对改变持怀疑态度时。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E7%94%9F%E7%89%A9%E7%A4%BE%E4%BC%9A%E7%90%86%E8%AE%BA%20psychoeducation%20%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97%E5%8C%96%E8%AE%B2%E4%B9%89%E4%BA%A4%E4%BB%98) · ★587)
- **DBT生物社会理论心理教育**: 在治疗同盟稳固后，以协作、隐喻化方式向来访者传授DBT生物社会理论核心图式（先天情绪敏感性 × 无效环境回应 → 调节技能发展受阻），促发归因重构与自我接纳，避免病理化自责。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E7%94%9F%E7%89%A9%E7%A4%BE%E4%BC%9A%E7%90%86%E8%AE%BA%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2) · ★587)
- **DBT疗法初始适配性评估与知情启动**: 在首次咨询前20分钟内，完成结构化保密说明、咨询原则传达、半结构化访谈、SCL-90/EPQ施测与简要反馈、四维自杀自伤风险探询，并基于中国常模与临床判断，协同来访者决策是否启动DBT取向咨询，签署知情同意。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95%E5%88%9D%E5%A7%8B%E9%80%82%E9%85%8D%E6%80%A7%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0%E4%B8%8E%E7%9F%A5%E6%83%85%E5%90%AF%E5%8A%A8) · ★587)
- **DBT目标作业单1引导式填写**: 使用标准化DBT目标作业单（作业单1），通过结构化提问引导来访者在首阶段具象化‘一个最想减少的可观察行为’与‘一项匹配的DBT替代技能’，完成行为-技能配对，聚焦具体、可操作、逻辑自洽的目标共建。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E7%9B%AE%E6%A0%87%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%8D%951%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC%E5%BC%8F%E5%A1%AB%E5%86%99) · ★587)
- **DBT第一阶段关系建立与正念锚定**: 在咨询初期（第1–4次）通过结构化正念作业单帮助来访者锚定当下、稳定注意力，同步建立安全治疗联盟。适用于来访者存在注意力涣散、情绪不稳、关系信任度低的初始阶段。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BB%BA%E7%AB%8B%E4%B8%8E%E6%AD%A3%E5%BF%B5%E9%94%9A%E5%AE%9A) · ★587)
- **DBT自伤行为安全优先响应规则**: 当DBT来访者报告或呈现新近自伤行为时，必须立即暂停技能教学，启动结构化三级响应：①确认当前安全状态；②完成行为链分析；③共同制定并签署24小时安全行为契约。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E8%87%AA%E4%BC%A4%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E5%AE%89%E5%85%A8%E4%BC%98%E5%85%88%E5%93%8D%E5%BA%94%E8%A7%84%E5%88%99) · ★587)
- **DBT行为链分析启动（自伤冲动阶段）**: 当来访者报告存在自伤冲动但未实施时，用于结构化探索该冲动前后的环境线索、情绪、想法、行为及后果，以识别可干预节点。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/DBT%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E9%93%BE%E5%88%86%E6%9E%90%E5%90%AF%E5%8A%A8%EF%BC%88%E8%87%AA%E4%BC%A4%E5%86%B2%E5%8A%A8%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%EF%BC%89) · ★587)
